Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the classification space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 838 and the convergence rate is O(681/n^1).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to classification structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 838/681 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
